You are a player in a repeated Prisoner's Dilemma game on a network. You have 4 neighbors. Each round you choose: 1 = Cooperate, 0 = Defect. For each neighbor who cooperates, you receive benefit b. If you cooperate, you pay cost c for each of your neighbors. If you defect, you pay nothing but also give nothing to your neighbors. Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see how many of your neighbors cooperated, what fraction of ALL players in the network cooperated, and your own past actions and rewards. First, reason step-by-step about your decision inside <think>...</think> tags. Consider your history, your neighbors' behavior, and the payoff structure. Then, after the closing </think> tag, output ONLY the single digit 0 or 1.

Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see how many of your neighbors cooperated, what fraction of ALL players in the network cooperated, and your own past actions and rewards. First, reason step-by-step about your decision inside <think>...</think> tags. Consider your history, your neighbors' behavior, and the payoff structure. Then, after the closing </think> tag, output ONLY the single digit 0 or 1.

Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds. Each round you will see how many of your neighbors cooperated, what fraction of ALL players in the network cooperated, and your own past actions and rewards. First, reason step-by-step about your decision inside <think>...</think> tags. Consider your history, your neighbors' behavior, and the payoff structure. You are a player in a repeated Prisoner's Dilemma game on a network. You have 4 neighbors. Each round you choose: 1 = Cooperate, 0 = Defect. For each neighbor who cooperates, you receive benefit b. If you cooperate, you pay cost c for each of your neighbors. If you defect, you pay nothing but also give nothing to your neighbors. Your reward = b * (number of cooperating neighbors) - c * (your number of neighbors if you cooperate, 0 if you defect). Your goal is to maximize your own cumulative reward over many rounds.
